# V 開頭詞條

> 本檔收錄 1,309 條含中文對譯的詞目。

<a id="entry-v-000001"></a>
## v

*n.* 1. **伏特** — a unit of potential equal to the potential difference between two points on a conductor carrying a current of 1 ampere when the power dissipated between the two points is 1 watt; equivalent to the potential difference across a resistance of 1 ohm when 1 ampere of current flows through it
2. **釩** — a soft silvery white toxic metallic element used in steel alloys; it occurs in several complex minerals including carnotite and vanadinite
3. **五** — the cardinal number that is the sum of four and one
4. the 22nd letter of the Roman alphabet
*adj.* 1. **五** — being one more than four

<a id="entry-v-000002"></a>
## V sign

*n.* 1. **V字手勢 /V字手勢** — A sign of victory or peace made by raising the index and middle fingers with the palm outwards.
2. *(Commonwealth, Ireland, UK)* **反V, V字手勢 /V字手勢** — A gesture of contempt or insult made by raising the index and middle fingers with the palm inwards; apocryphally originated by English bowmen at Agincourt (in actuality attested only from 1901).

<a id="entry-v-000003"></a>
## v-1

*n.* 1. **自動操縱導彈** — a small jet-propelled winged missile that carries a bomb

<a id="entry-v-000004"></a>
## V-neck

*n.* 1. **V領 /V領** — A V-shaped neckline on a garment such as a pullover, usually at the front of the garment.

<a id="entry-v-000005"></a>
## v-shaped

*adj.* 1. **V形狀+的** — shaped in the form of the letter V

<a id="entry-v-000006"></a>
## vacancy

**發音：** /ˈveɪkənsi/
*n.* 1. **空閒** — being unoccupied
2. **真空, 空, 空處, 空隙** — an empty area or space
*“the huge desert voids”*
*“the emptiness of outer space”*
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **空缺, 空職 /空職, 空額 /空額** — An unoccupied position or job.
4. *(countable, uncountable)* **空位** — An available room in a hotel; guest house, etc.

<a id="entry-v-000007"></a>
## vacancy rate

*n.* 1. **空房率, 空缺率** — the percentage of all rental units (as in hotels) that are unoccupied or not rented at a given time

<a id="entry-v-000008"></a>
## vacant

**發音：** /ˈveɪkənt/
*adj.* 1. **頭腦空虛+的, 茫然+的** — void of thought or knowledge
*“a vacant mind”*
2. **空的** — Not occupied; empty.
3. without an occupant or incumbent
*“the throne is never vacant”*

<a id="entry-v-000009"></a>
## vacate

*v.* 1. **辭去, 辭職, 離開** — leave (a job, post, or position) voluntarily
*“She vacated the position when she got pregnant”*
*“The chairman resigned when he was found to have misappropriated funds”*
2. **使空出, 騰出, 搬出** — leave behind empty; move out of
*“You must vacate your office by tonight”*
3. **逆轉, 廢止, 顛倒, 撤回, 撤消** — cancel officially
*“He revoked the ban on smoking”*
*“lift an embargo”*

<a id="entry-v-000010"></a>
## vacation

**發音：** /vəˈkeɪ.ʃən/
*n.* 1. **休息日, 休假, 假期, 假日** — leisure time away from work devoted to rest or pleasure
*“we get two weeks of vacation every summer”*
*“we took a short holiday in Puerto Rico”*
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **假期** — An extended period of leisure time away from work or school.
3. the act of making something legally void
*v.* 1. **度假, 休假** — spend or take a vacation

<a id="entry-v-000011"></a>
## vaccina

*n.* 1. **牛痘** — a local infection induced in humans by inoculation with the virus causing cowpox in order to confer resistance to smallpox; normally lasts three weeks and leaves a pitted scar

<a id="entry-v-000012"></a>
## vaccinate

**發音：** /ˈvæksɪneɪt/
*v.* 1. *(transitive)* **接種 /接種** — To treat (a person or an animal) with a vaccine to produce immunity against a disease.
2. perform vaccinations or produce immunity in by inoculation
*“We vaccinate against scarlet fever”*
*“The nurse vaccinated the children in the school”*

<a id="entry-v-000013"></a>
## vaccinated

*adj.* 1. **免疫了+的, 接種了+的** — having been rendered unsusceptible to a disease

<a id="entry-v-000014"></a>
## vaccination

**發音：** /ˌvæk.sɪˈneɪ.ʃən/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **疫苗接種 /疫苗接種, 預防接種 /預防接種** — Inoculation with a vaccine, in order to protect from a particular disease or strain of disease.
2. **接種, 種痘** — the scar left following inoculation with a vaccine
3. taking a vaccine as a precaution against contracting a disease

<a id="entry-v-000015"></a>
## vaccine

**發音：** /vækˈsin/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **疫苗** — A substance given to stimulate a body's production of antibodies and provide immunity against a disease without causing the disease itself in the treatment, prepared from the agent that causes the disease (or a derivative of it; or a related, also effective, but safer disease), or a synthetic substitute; also, a dose of such a substance.
2. immunogen consisting of a suspension of weakened or dead pathogenic cells injected in order to stimulate the production of antibodies

<a id="entry-v-000016"></a>
## vaccine hesitancy

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **疫苗猶豫 /疫苗猶豫, 疫苗猶豫** — Reluctance to get vaccinated, often leading to refusal to be vaccinated.

<a id="entry-v-000017"></a>
## vaccine passport

*n.* 1. **疫苗護照 /疫苗護照** — A document that proves vaccination against (certain) infectious diseases, particularly for the purpose of international travel.

<a id="entry-v-000018"></a>
## Vaccine Revolt

*prop. n.* 1. *(historical)* **疫苗革命** — A period of civil disorder that occurred from 10 to 16 November 1904, in the city of Rio de Janeiro, Brazil, in response to government officials entering homes to administer smallpox vaccines by force.

<a id="entry-v-000019"></a>
## vaccinia

*n.* 1. **牛痘** — a local infection induced in humans by inoculation with the virus causing cowpox in order to confer resistance to smallpox; normally lasts three weeks and leaves a pitted scar
2. **牛痘** — a viral disease of cattle causing a mild skin disease affecting the udder; formerly used to inoculate humans against smallpox

<a id="entry-v-000020"></a>
## vaccinia gangrenosa

*n.* 1. **進行性痘疹** — a severe or even fatal form of vaccinia that occurs mainly in persons with an immunological deficiency; characterized by progressive enlargement of the initial lesion

<a id="entry-v-000021"></a>
## vaccinium macrocarpon

*n.* 1. **大果越桔** — trailing red-fruited plant

<a id="entry-v-000022"></a>
## vacillant

*adj.* 1. **猶豫+的, 優柔寡斷+的, 猶豫不決+的, 躊躇+的** — uncertain in purpose or action

<a id="entry-v-000023"></a>
## vacillate

**發音：** /ˈvæ.sə.leɪt/
*v.* 1. *(intransitive)* **搖擺不定 /搖擺不定** — To sway unsteadily from one side to the other; oscillate.
2. **彷徨, 搖擺不定, 徘徊, 猶豫, 搖擺** — be undecided about something; waver between conflicting positions or courses of action
*“He oscillates between accepting the new position and retirement”*
3. *(intransitive)* **猶豫不定** — To swing indecisively from one course of action or opinion to another.
4. move or sway in a rising and falling or wavelike pattern
*“the line on the monitor vacillated”*

<a id="entry-v-000024"></a>
## vacillating

*adj.* 1. **猶豫+的, 優柔寡斷+的, 猶豫不決+的, 躊躇+的** — uncertain in purpose or action

<a id="entry-v-000025"></a>
## vacillation

*n.* 1. **擺動, 搖擺, 遊移不定** — changing location by moving back and forth
2. indecision in speech or action

<a id="entry-v-000026"></a>
## vacuity

*n.* 1. **缺乏** — the absence of matter
2. a region that is devoid of matter
3. total lack of meaning or ideas

<a id="entry-v-000027"></a>
## vacuolate

*adj.* 1. **含有空泡+的, 有液泡+的, 有空泡+的, 空泡+的** — formed into or containing one or more vacuoles or small membrane-bound cavities within a cell

<a id="entry-v-000028"></a>
## vacuolated

*adj.* 1. **含有空泡+的, 有液泡+的, 有空泡+的, 空泡+的** — formed into or containing one or more vacuoles or small membrane-bound cavities within a cell

<a id="entry-v-000029"></a>
## vacuolation

*n.* 1. **形成空泡, 空泡形成** — the state of having become filled with vacuoles

<a id="entry-v-000030"></a>
## vacuole

**發音：** /ˈvækjʊˌəʊl/
*n.* 1. **液泡** — A large membrane-bound vesicle in a cell's cytoplasm.
2. a tiny cavity filled with fluid in the cytoplasm of a cell

<a id="entry-v-000031"></a>
## vacuolisation

*n.* 1. **形成空泡, 空泡形成** — the state of having become filled with vacuoles

<a id="entry-v-000032"></a>
## vacuolization

*n.* 1. **形成空泡, 空泡形成** — the state of having become filled with vacuoles

<a id="entry-v-000033"></a>
## vacuous

*adj.* 1. **愚笨+的, 昏庸+的, 愚蠢+的** — devoid of intelligence
2. devoid of significance or point
*“empty promises”*
*“a hollow victory”*
3. devoid of matter
*“a vacuous space”*
4. void of expression
*“a blank stare”*

<a id="entry-v-000034"></a>
## vacuum

**發音：** /ˈvæ.kjuːm/
*n.* 1. **缺乏** — the absence of matter
2. **真空** — A region of space that contains no matter.
3. **真空, 空, 空處, 空隙** — an empty area or space
*“the huge desert voids”*
*“the emptiness of outer space”*
4. **吸塵器, 真空吸塵器** — an electrical home appliance that cleans by suction
5. a region that is devoid of matter
*v.* 1. *(intransitive)* **吸塵 /吸塵** — To use a vacuum cleaner.
2. clean with a vacuum cleaner
*“vacuum the carpets”*

<a id="entry-v-000035"></a>
## vacuum bag

*n.* 1. **集塵袋** — a bag into which dirt is sucked by a vacuum cleaner

<a id="entry-v-000036"></a>
## vacuum bed

*n.* 1. **真空床** — A device consisting of a large, latex envelope attached to a frame, from which most of the air may be removed via a vacuum pump while a person lies inside, which heavily restricts movement and induces asphyxiation for the purposes of stimulation.

<a id="entry-v-000037"></a>
## vacuum chamber

*n.* 1. *(US)* **真空室** — A compartment which can be depressurized to the point where no air exists inside of it. (This entry is a translation hub.)
2. a chamber from which nearly all matter (especially air) has been removed

<a id="entry-v-000038"></a>
## vacuum cleaner

*n.* 1. **吸塵器, 真空吸塵器** — an electrical home appliance that cleans by suction
2. **吸塵器 /吸塵器, 真空吸塵器 /真空吸塵器** — A device that uses suction, and often agitation, in order to remove dirt and other debris from carpets and hard floors.

<a id="entry-v-000039"></a>
## vacuum pump

*n.* 1. **真空泵** — A piece of equipment used for extraction of gas or vapour from an enclosed space, leaving the container with a partial vacuum.
2. **抽氣機, 真空泵** — a pump that moves air in or out of something

<a id="entry-v-000040"></a>
## vacuum tube

*n.* 1. **真空管, 電子管 /電子管** — A type of electron tube that controls electron current through vacuum between electrodes within a sealed container, usually of glass or metal and often designed to be easily installed in and removed from the equipment in which it is used.
2. electronic device consisting of a system of electrodes arranged in an evacuated glass or metal envelope

<a id="entry-v-000041"></a>
## Vadodara

*prop. n.* 1. **巴羅達 /巴羅達** — A large city and district of Gujarat, India (formerly Baroda).

<a id="entry-v-000042"></a>
## Vaduz

**發音：** /vəˈduːts/
*prop. n.* 1. **瓦杜茲 /瓦杜茲** — A town and municipality, the capital of Liechtenstein.

<a id="entry-v-000043"></a>
## vagabond

**發音：** /ˈvæ.ɡə.bɒnd/
*n.* 1. **浪人** — A person on a trip of indeterminate destination and/or length of time.
2. **流浪漢 /流浪漢** — One who usually wanders from place to place, having no fixed dwelling, or not abiding in it, and usually without the means of honest livelihood.
3. anything that resembles a vagabond in having no fixed place
*“pirate ships were vagabonds of the sea”*
4. a wanderer who has no established residence or visible means of support
*v.* 1. **遊蕩, 漫遊, 流浪, 徘徊, 漫步** — move about aimlessly or without any destination, often in search of food or employment
*“The gypsies roamed the woods”*
*“roving vagabonds”*
*adj.* 1. **浪蕩+的, 流浪+的, 漂泊+的** — wandering aimlessly without ties to a place or community
*“led a vagabond life”*
*“a rootless wanderer”*
2. **流浪+的, 流浪者+的, 遊蕩+的, 漂泊+的** — continually changing especially as from one abode or occupation to another
*“a drifting double-dealer”*
*“the floating population”*

<a id="entry-v-000044"></a>
## vagal

*adj.* 1. **迷走神經+的** — of or relating to the vagus nerve

<a id="entry-v-000045"></a>
## vaginal

**發音：** /vəˈd͡ʒʌɪ.nəl/
*adj.* 1. *(not-comparable, relational)* **陰道的 /陰道的** — Of, relating to, or affecting the vagina.
2. **陰道+的** — of or relating to the vagina
*“vaginal suppository”*
3. *(not-comparable, relational)* **鞘的** — Of or relating to a theca.

<a id="entry-v-000046"></a>
## vaginal birth

*n.* 1. **娩, 生產, 出生, 分娩** — the parturition process in human beings; having a baby; the process of giving birth to a child

<a id="entry-v-000047"></a>
## vaginal discharge

*n.* 1. **陰道分泌物, 陰道溢液** — discharge of secretions from the cervical glands of the vagina; normally clear or white

<a id="entry-v-000048"></a>
## vaginal fluid

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **陰道分泌物 /陰道分泌物, 白帶 /白帶** — Any liquid discharge from a vagina.

<a id="entry-v-000049"></a>
## vaginal process

*n.* 1. *(archaic)* **鞘突** — A projecting lamina of bone on the inferior surface of the petrous portion of the temporal bone that is continuous with the tympanic plate and surrounds the base of the styloid process.
2. **鞘突** — An outpouching of peritoneum through the inguinal canal, from which the serous membrane (tunica vaginalis) covering the testicle is derived.
3. **鞘突** — Either of a pair of projecting plates on the inferior surface of the sphenoid bone which articulate with the alae of the vomer.

<a id="entry-v-000050"></a>
## vaginal ring

*n.* 1. **陰道環 /陰道環** — A device consisting of a flexible ring held in the vagina which provides a controlled release of drugs, typically used for contraception.

<a id="entry-v-000051"></a>
## vaginal sex

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **陰道性交 /陰道性交** — Sexual intercourse in which the penis is inserted into the vagina.

<a id="entry-v-000052"></a>
## vaginismus

**發音：** /væd͡ʒɪˈnɪzməs/
*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **陰道痙攣 /陰道痙攣** — A painful muscular contraction of the vagina when attempting to insert something, such as a tampon or a penis, into it.
2. **陰道痙攣** — muscular contraction that causes the vagina to close; usually an anxiety reaction before coitus or pelvic examination

<a id="entry-v-000053"></a>
## vaginitis

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **陰道炎 /陰道炎** — Inflammation of the vagina.
2. **陰道炎** — inflammation of the vagina (usually associated with candidiasis)

<a id="entry-v-000054"></a>
## vaginocele

*n.* 1. **陰道疝, 陰道脫垂** — hernia projecting into the vagina

<a id="entry-v-000055"></a>
## vaginoplasty

*n.* 1. **陰道成形術 /陰道成形術** — A surgical procedure used to construct or reconstruct a vaginal canal and mucous membrane.

<a id="entry-v-000056"></a>
## vaginosis

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **陰道病 /陰道病** — Any disease or infection of the vagina.

<a id="entry-v-000057"></a>
## vagrancy

*n.* 1. **流浪, 漂泊** — the state of wandering from place to place; having no permanent home or means of livelihood

<a id="entry-v-000058"></a>
## vagrant

**發音：** /ˈveɪɡɹənt/
*n.* 1. *(dated)* **流浪漢 /流浪漢, 遊民 /遊民** — A person who wanders from place to place; a nomad, a wanderer.
2. *(specifically)* **江湖, 浪人, 流丐, 流浪漢 /流浪漢, 遊民 /遊民, 無業遊民 /無業遊民** — A person without settled employment or habitation who usually supports himself or herself by begging or some dishonest means; a tramp, a vagabond.
3. **彩蛺蝶屬 /彩蛺蝶屬** — Any member of the species Vagrans egista of widely distributed Asian butteflies of the family Nymphalidae.
4. *(especially)* **迷鳥 /迷鳥** — An animal, typically a bird, found outside its species' usual range.
5. a wanderer who has no established residence or visible means of support
*adj.* 1. **流浪+的, 流浪者+的, 遊蕩+的, 漂泊+的** — continually changing especially as from one abode or occupation to another
*“a drifting double-dealer”*
*“the floating population”*

<a id="entry-v-000059"></a>
## vague

**發音：** /veɪɡ/
*adj.* 1. **晦澀+的, 含糊+的, 難解+的, 不明確+的, 不清楚+的** — not clearly understood or expressed
*“an obscure turn of phrase”*
*“an impulse to go off and fight certain obscure battles of his own spirit"-Anatole Broyard”*
2. **未下定義+的, 不明確+的, 不確定+的, 模糊+的** — not precisely limited, determined, or distinguished
*“an undefined term”*
*“undefined authority”*
3. **不明顯的, 不明確的, 含糊+的, 模糊不清+的, 不清楚+的** — lacking clarity or distinctness
*“a dim figure in the distance”*
*“only a faint recollection”*
4. **模糊, 糢糊** — Not clearly expressed; stated in indefinite terms.

<a id="entry-v-000060"></a>
## vagueness

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **含糊** — The condition of being imprecise or unclear in meaning: vague.
2. unclearness by virtue of being poorly expressed or not coherent in meaning
*“the Conservative manifesto is a model of vagueness”*
*“these terms were used with a vagueness that suggested little or no thought about what each might convey”*
3. indistinctness of shape or character
*“the scene had the swirling vagueness of a painting by Turner”*

<a id="entry-v-000061"></a>
## vagus nerve

*n.* 1. **迷走神經 /迷走神經** — Either of the tenth pair of cranial nerves which extend from the medulla oblongata down into the abdomen. Branches of these important nerves supply the tongue, larynx, lungs, gut, and heart.
2. a mixed nerve that supplies the pharynx and larynx and lungs and heart and esophagus and stomach and most of the abdominal viscera

<a id="entry-v-000062"></a>
## Vahagn

**發音：** /vəˈhɑ(ː).ɡən/
*prop. n.* 1. *(Armenian)* **瓦哈格恩** — Ancient Armenian god of war, fire and courage. A dragonslayer. Lover of Astghik.
2. **瓦哈格恩** — A transliteration of the Armenian male given name Վահագն (Vahagn).

<a id="entry-v-000063"></a>
## vain

**發音：** /veɪn/
*adj.* 1. **自命不凡+的, 自我中心的, 自我本位+的, 自負+的, 趾高氣揚+的** — characteristic of false pride; having an exaggerated sense of self-importance
*“a conceited fool”*
*“an attitude of self-conceited arrogance”*
2. **虛榮 /虛榮** — Overly proud of oneself, especially concerning appearance; having a high opinion of one's own accomplishments with slight reason.
3. **白白, 徒然, 徒勞 /徒勞** — Effecting no purpose; pointless, futile.

## value

**發音：** /ˈvæl.juː/
*n.* 1. **值, 數值** — a numerical quantity measured or assigned or computed
*“the value assigned was 16 milliseconds”*
2. **估價, 評價, 價值** — the amount (of money or goods or services) that is considered to be a fair equivalent for something else
*“he tried to estimate the value of the produce at normal prices”*
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **值** — Any definite numerical quantity or other mathematical object, determined by being measured, computed, or otherwise defined.
4. *(countable, uncountable)* **價值 /價值** — The quality that renders something desirable or valuable; worth.
5. **價值** — an ideal accepted by some individual or group
*“he has old-fashioned values”*
6. the quality (positive or negative) that renders something desirable or valuable
*“the Shakespearean Shylock is of dubious value in the modern world”*
*v.* 1. **珍愛, 珍視, 珍重, 重視** — hold dear
*“I prize these old photographs”*
2. **尊重, 重視, 遵守** — regard highly; think much of
*“I respect his judgement”*
*“We prize his creativity”*
3. **估量, 評價, 評估, 估價, 評判** — evaluate or estimate the nature, quality, ability, extent, or significance of
*“I will have the family jewels appraised by a professional”*
*“access all the factors when taking a risk”*
4. fix or determine the value of; assign a value to
*“value the jewelry and art work in the estate”*
5. estimate the value of
*“How would you rate his chances to become President?”*
*“Gold was rated highly among the Romans”*

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **風險價值 /風險價值** — A widely used measure of the risk of loss on a specific portfolio of financial assets. For a given portfolio, probability and time horizon, VaR is a threshold value such that the probability that the mark-to-market loss on the portfolio over the given time horizon exceeds this value (assuming normal markets and no trading) is the given probability level.

<a id="entry-v-000228"></a>
## variable

**發音：** [ˈvɛə.ɹi.ə.bl̩]
*adj.* 1. **可變+的, 變化無常+的, 多變+的, 易變+的** — liable to or capable of change
*“rainfall in the tropics is notoriously variable”*
*“variable winds”*
2. **變化+的, 易變+的, 變化無常+的, 多變+的** — marked by diversity or difference
*“the varying angles of roof slope”*
*“nature is infinitely variable”*
3. **變數 /變數** — Having no fixed quantitative value.
4. (used of a device) designed so that a property (as e.g. light) can be varied
*“a variable capacitor”*
*“variable filters in front of the mercury xenon lights”*
*n.* 1. **變數, 變量** — a quantity that can assume any of a set of values
2. **變數 /變數, 變量 /變量** — A quantity that may assume any one of a set of values.
3. **變量 /變量** — A named memory location in which a program can store intermediate results and from which it can read them.
4. something that is likely to vary; something that is subject to variation
*“the weather is one variable to be considered”*
5. a star that varies noticeably in brightness
6. a symbol (like x or y) that is used in mathematical or logical expressions to represent a variable quantity

*n.* 1. **織女星人 /織女星人** — Someone from the vicinity of the star Vega.

<a id="entry-v-000330"></a>
## vegan

**發音：** /ˈviːɡən/
*n.* 1. **純素食者** — A person committed to avoiding products and practices that inherently involve animal use, including all foods containing animal products, and to abstaining from direct and intentional harm to animals as far as possible; an adherent to veganism.
2. a strict vegetarian; someone who eats no animal or dairy products at all
*adj.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **純素 /純素** — Relating to vegans or veganism.

<a id="entry-v-000331"></a>
## veganism

*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **純素食主義 /純素食主義, 維根主義 /維根主義, 純素主義 /純素主義, 嚴格素食主義 /嚴格素食主義, 完全素食主義 /完全素食主義** — Strict vegetarianism; the practice of eating neither meat nor other animal products, such as fish, milk and milk products, eggs, and honey.

**發音：** /ˈve.d͡ʒiː.mɑɪt/

<a id="entry-v-000333"></a>
## vegetable

**發音：** /ˈvɛd͡ʒ.tə.bəl/
*n.* 1. **蔬菜, 青菜** — edible seeds or roots or stems or leaves or bulbs or tubers or nonsweet fruits of any of numerous herbaceous plant
2. **蔬菜, 青菜** — A plant raised for some edible part of it, such as the leaves, roots, fruit or flowers, but excluding any plant considered to be a fruit, grain, herb, or spice in the culinary sense, and usually including non-plants lumped into the same culinary category, such as edible mushrooms and seaweed.
3. *(archaic)* **植物** — Any plant.
4. *(derogatory, figuratively, often)* **植物人** — A person whose brain (or, infrequently, whose body) has been damaged to the point that they cannot interact with the surrounding environment; a person in a persistent vegetative state.
5. any of various herbaceous plants cultivated for an edible part such as the fruit or the root of the beet or the leaf of spinach or the seeds of bean plants or the flower buds of broccoli or cauliflower
*adj.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **蔬菜** — Of or relating to plants.

<a id="entry-v-000334"></a>

*n.* 1. **過溝菜蕨 /過溝菜蕨** — Diplazium esculentum; an edible fern found throughout Asia and Oceania.

<a id="entry-v-000345"></a>

<a id="entry-v-000346"></a>

**發音：** /vɛd͡ʒɪˈtɛɹi.ənɪzəm/

2. a diet excluding all meat and fish

<a id="entry-v-000347"></a>
## vegetate

*v.* 1. **茫然地過日子, 過單調乏味的生話, 過單調呆板的生活, 過著單調的生活** — lead a passive existence without using one's body or mind
2. establish vegetation on
*“They vegetated the hills behind their house”*
3. produce vegetation
*“The fields vegetate vigorously”*
4. grow like a plant
*“This fungus usually vegetates vigorously”*
5. grow or spread abnormally
*“warts and polyps can vegetate if not removed”*
6. propagate asexually
*“The bacterial growth vegetated along”*

<a id="entry-v-000348"></a>
## vegetation

**發音：** /ˌvɛd͡ʒəˈteɪʃən/
*n.* 1. **植物群, 植被, 草木** — all the plant life in a particular region or period
*“Pleistocene vegetation”*
*“the flora of southern California”*
2. *(uncountable)* **植被, 草木** — Plants, taken collectively.
3. **植物生長** — the process of growth in plants
4. an abnormal growth or excrescence (especially a warty excrescence on the valves of the heart)
5. inactivity that is passive and monotonous, comparable to the inactivity of plant life
*“their holiday was spent in sleep and vegetation”*

<a id="entry-v-000349"></a>
## vegetational

*adj.* 1. **有關植物生長+的, 植物性+的, 植物+的** — composed of vegetation or plants
*“regions rich in vegetal products”*
*“vegetational cover”*

<a id="entry-v-000350"></a>
## vegetative

*adj.* 1. **植物人狀態+的, 生活呆板單調+的** — of or relating to an activity that is passive and monotonous
*“a dull vegetative lifestyle”*
2. **有關植物生長+的, 植物性+的, 植物+的** — composed of vegetation or plants
*“regions rich in vegetal products”*
*“vegetational cover”*
3. **植物性+的** — relating to involuntary bodily functions
*“vegetative functions such as digestion or growth or circulation”*
4. **無性生殖+的, 植物性+的** — (of reproduction) characterized by asexual processes

<a id="entry-v-000351"></a>

*“the storm's violence”*

<a id="entry-v-000355"></a>

*“vehement deluges of rain”*

<a id="entry-v-000357"></a>
## vehicle

**發音：** /ˈviː.ɪ.kəl/
*n.* 1. **車輛** — a conveyance that transports people or objects
2. **車輛 /車輛** — A conveyance: a spacious device for carrying or transporting substances, objects, people or animals.
3. **媒介** — A medium for expression of talent or views.
4. a medium for the expression or achievement of something
*“his editorials provided a vehicle for his political views”*
*“a congregation is a vehicle of group identity”*
5. any substance that facilitates the use of a drug or pigment or other material that is mixed with it
6. any inanimate object (as a towel or money or clothing or dishes or books or toys etc.) that can transmit infectious agents from one person to another

*“a hidden message”*
*“a veiled threat”*

2. **面紗 /面紗** — A covering for a person or thing; as, a caul (especially over the head)
3. **披肩** — a vestment worn by a priest at High Mass in the Roman Catholic Church; a silk shawl

## vein

**發音：** /veɪn/
*n.* 1. **血管, 靜脈** — a blood vessel that carries blood from the capillaries toward the heart
*“all veins except the pulmonary vein carry unaerated blood”*

3. **葉脈** — any of the vascular bundles or ribs that form the branching framework of conducting and supporting tissues in a leaf or other plant organ
4. **翅脈** — The nervure of an insect’s wing.
5. **翅脈** — one of the horny ribs that stiffen and support the wing of an insect
6. **靜脈 /靜脈** — A blood vessel that transports blood from the capillaries back to the heart.
*v.* 1. make a veinlike pattern

*“leaves affected with veinal mosaic”*

**發音：** /ˈvɛn ˌdaɪəɡɹæm/
*n.* 1. **文氏圖 /文氏圖, 維恩圖 /維恩圖** — A diagram representing some sets by contours of closed shapes, such as circles or ellipses (and sometimes also the universal set as a rectangle enclosing all of these shapes), and indicating the relationships between the sets: by overlapping the shapes to show that the corresponding sets have a non-empty intersection, and by possibly (but not necessarily) enclosing all of the sets (which are proper subsets of the universal set) within a universal set (represented typically by a rectangle); such that the total number of simply connected regions is 2ⁿ, where n is the number of depicted sets which are proper subsets of the universal set.

*n.* 1. a hole for the escape of gas or air

## ventilate

*v.* 1. **使通風, 使換氣, 使空氣流通** — expose to the circulation of fresh air so as to retard spoilage
*“Wheat should be well ventilated”*
2. **使換氣, 使通風, 使空氣流通** — circulate through and freshen
*“The gust of air ventilated the room”*
3. expose to cool or cold air so as to cool or freshen
*“air the old winter clothes”*
*“air out the smoke-filled rooms”*
4. give expression or utterance to
*“She vented her anger”*
*“The graduates gave vent to cheers”*
5. furnish with an opening to allow air to circulate or gas to escape
*“The architect did not think about ventilating the storage space”*

<a id="entry-v-000434"></a>
## ventilated

*“a well ventilated room”*

<a id="entry-v-000435"></a>
## ventilation

**發音：** /ˌvɛntɪˈleɪʃ(ə)n/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **通風 /通風, 通氣 /通氣** — The replacement of stale or noxious air with fresh.
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **換氣 /換氣, 呼吸** — The bodily process of breathing; the inhalation of air to provide oxygen, and the exhalation of spent air to remove carbon dioxide.
3. the act of supplying fresh air and getting rid of foul air
4. a mechanical system in a building that provides fresh air
*“she was continually adjusting the ventilation”*
5. free and open discussion of (or debate on) some question of public interest
*“such a proposal deserves thorough public discussion”*
6. the bodily process of inhalation and exhalation; the process of taking in oxygen from inhaled air and releasing carbon dioxide by exhalation

<a id="entry-v-000436"></a>

**發音：** /ˈvɛntɪleɪtə/

2. **呼吸器** — a device that facilitates breathing in cases of respiratory failure
3. **通風器 /通風器, 換氣機 /換氣機** — A device that circulates fresh air and expels stale or noxious air.
4. a device (such as a fan) that introduces fresh air or expels foul air

<a id="entry-v-000437"></a>

## venture

*v.* 1. **冒險行動, 敢於, 冒險** — proceed somewhere despite the risk of possible dangers
*“We ventured into the world of high-tech and bought a supercomputer”*
2. **冒險, 推測** — put forward, of a guess, in spite of possible refutation
*“I am guessing that the price of real estate will rise again”*
*“I cannot pretend to say that you are wrong”*
3. **大膽進行, 敢於, 冒險** — put at risk
*“I will stake my good reputation for this”*
*n.* 1. **冒險事業, 冒險行動** — any venturesome undertaking especially one with an uncertain outcome
2. **投機事業, 投機生意, 高風險投資, 投機** — an investment that is very risky but could yield great profits
*“he knew the stock was a speculation when he bought it”*
3. **投機, 冒險** — a commercial undertaking that risks a loss but promises a profit

## verb-framing

<a id="entry-v-000479"></a>
## verbal

**發音：** /ˈvɜː.bəl/
*adj.* 1. **用詞語表達+的, 言語+的, 言辭+的, 詞語+的** — of or relating to or formed from words in general
*“verbal ability”*
2. *(not-comparable)* **詞的 /詞的** — Of or relating to words.
3. **動詞+的, 動詞派生+的** — of or relating to or formed from a verb
*“verbal adjectives like `running' in `hot and cold running water'”*
4. **措辭+的, 文字上+的, 用文字+的, 用言辭+的** — relating to or having facility in the use of words
*“a good poet is a verbal artist”*

*“a verbal contract”*
6. communicated in the form of words
*“verbal imagery”*
*“a verbal protest”*

**發音：** /ˈvɜː.tɪ.kəl/

*“a vertical camera angle”*

*“an erect stature”*

## vex

*v.* 1. **使煩惱, 惹惱, 打攪, 折磨, 激怒** — cause annoyance in; disturb, especially by minor irritations
*“Mosquitoes buzzing in my ear really bothers me”*
*“It irritates me that she never closes the door after she leaves”*
2. **使重新組合, 改組** — change the arrangement or position of
3. disturb the peace of mind of; afflict with mental agitation or distress
*“I cannot sleep--my daughter's health is worrying me”*
4. subject to prolonged examination, discussion, or deliberation
*“vex the subject of the death penalty”*

*“Got me--I don't know the answer!”*

*n.* 1. **西哥特人, 西哥德人** — Any member of an ancient East Germanic tribe, one branch of the Goths (the Ostrogoths being the other), which participated in several wars with Rome and established a kingdom with Toulouse for its capital.

<a id="entry-v-000976"></a>
## vision

**發音：** /ˈvɪʒ.ən/
*n.* 1. **幻象** — a vivid mental image
*“he had a vision of his own death”*
2. **眼力, 視力, 視覺** — the ability to see; the visual faculty
3. *(uncountable)* **視力 /視力, 視覺 /視覺, 眼力** — The sense or ability of sight.
4. *(countable)* **前景, 願景 /願景, 展望** — Something imaginary one thinks one sees.
5. *(countable)* **展望** — An ideal or a goal toward which one aspires.
6. *(countable)* **異象 /異象** — A religious or mystical experience of a supernatural appearance.

<a id="entry-v-000977"></a>
## vision defect

*n.* 1. **視覺障礙** — impairment of the sense of sight

<a id="entry-v-000978"></a>
## visionary

*n.* 1. a person given to fanciful speculations and enthusiasms with little regard for what is actually possible
2. a person with unusual powers of foresight
*adj.* 1. **不實際+的, 不現實+的, 不能實行+的, 夢想+的, 幻影+的** — not practical or realizable; speculative
*“airy theories about socioeconomic improvement”*
*“visionary schemes for getting rich”*

<a id="entry-v-000979"></a>
## visit

**發音：** /ˈvɪzɪt/
*v.* 1. **遊覽, 視察, 參觀, 訪問** — go to see a place, as for entertainment
*“We went to see the Eiffel Tower in the morning”*
2. **參觀, 看望, 訪問** — go to certain places as for sightseeing
*“Did you ever visit Paris?”*
3. **拜訪** — pay a brief visit
*“The mayor likes to call on some of the prominent citizens”*
4. *(intransitive, transitive)* **訪問 /訪問, 看望, 參觀 /參觀, 來訪 /來訪** — To go and meet (a person) as an act of friendliness or sociability.
5. **暫住** — stay with as a guest
*“Every summer, we visited our relatives in the country for a month”*
6. come to see in an official or professional capacity
*“The governor visited the prison”*
*“The grant administrator visited the laboratory”*
*n.* 1. **作客, 參觀, 拜訪, 看望, 訪問** — the act of going to see some person or place or thing for a short time
*“he dropped by for a visit”*
2. **訪問 /訪問, 往訪 /往訪, 來訪 /來訪** — A single act of visiting.
3. a meeting arranged by the visitor to see someone (such as a doctor or lawyer) for treatment or advice
*“he scheduled a visit to the dentist”*
4. the act of visiting in an official capacity (as for an inspection)
5. the act of going to see some person in a professional capacity
*“a visit to the dentist”*
6. a temporary stay (e.g., as a guest)

<a id="entry-v-000980"></a>
## visitant

*n.* 1. **來訪者, 訪客, 客人** — someone who visits

<a id="entry-v-000981"></a>
## visitation

*n.* 1. **麻煩事, 考驗** — an annoying or frustrating or catastrophic event
*“his mother-in-law's visits were a great trial for him”*
*“life is full of tribulations”*
2. any disaster or catastrophe
*“a visitation of the plague”*
3. an official visit for inspection or supervision
*“the commissioner made visitations to all the precinct stations”*
*“the recent visitation of the bishop to his diocese”*

<a id="entry-v-000982"></a>
## visiting card

*n.* 1. **名片** — a printed or written greeting that is left to indicate that you have visited

<a id="entry-v-000983"></a>

<a id="entry-v-000984"></a>

3. *(specifically)* **遊客 /遊客, 客人, 訪客 /訪客, 觀光客 /觀光客** — An extraterrestrial being on Earth.

*“he pulled down the bill of his cap and trudged ahead”*
2. **帽舌, 遮陽板 /遮陽板** — The fore piece of a cap, projecting over, and protecting the eyes.

<a id="entry-v-001006"></a>
## visualise

*v.* 1. **使顯現, 使看得見, 顯影, 現形, 使具體化** — view the outline of by means of an X-ray
*“The radiologist can visualize the cancerous liver”*
2. form a mental picture of something that is invisible or abstract
*“Mathematicians often visualize”*
3. imagine; conceive of; see in one's mind
*“I can't see him on horseback!”*
*“I can see what will happen”*
4. make visible
*“With this machine, ultrasound can be visualized”*

## vital

**發音：** /ˈvaɪtəl/
*adj.* 1. **緊要+的, 關鍵+的, 重要+的** — urgently needed; absolutely necessary
*“a critical element of the plan”*
*“critical medical supplies”*
2. **生命的** — Relating to or characteristic of life.
3. **重要** — Very important.
4. performing an essential function in the living body
*“vital organs”*
*“blood and other vital fluids”*
5. full of spirit
*“a dynamic full of life woman”*
*“a vital and charismatic leader”*
6. manifesting or characteristic of life
*“a vital, living organism”*
*“vital signs”*

<a id="entry-v-001015"></a>
## vital capacity

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **肺活量** — The maximum volume of air that can be discharged from the lungs following maximum inspiration.
2. **肺活量** — the maximum amount of air that can be exhaled after a maximum inhalation (usually tested with a spirometer); used to determine the condition of lung tissue

<a id="entry-v-001016"></a>
## vital organ

*n.* 1. **生命器官** — An organ of the body which performs a sufficiently important function that damage to the organ can cause death.
2. a bodily organ that is essential for life

<a id="entry-v-001017"></a>
## vital sign

*n.* 1. **生命體徵, 生命徵兆** — sign of life; usually an indicator of a person's general physical condition
*“he was still alive but his vital signs were weak”*

<a id="entry-v-001018"></a>
## vital signs

*n.* 1. *(plural, plural-only)* **生命跡象 /生命跡象, 生命徵象 /生命徵象, 生命體徵 /生命體徵** — A set of measurements of a patient's condition taken regularly to assess the state of bodily functions: CSM, skin signs, lung sounds, temperature, pulse, blood pressure, and respiration.

<a id="entry-v-001019"></a>
## vital statistics

*n.* 1. *(plural, plural-only)* **三圍 /三圍** — The size of a woman's bust, waist and hips, normally measured in inches.
2. data relating to births and deaths and health and diseases and marriages

<a id="entry-v-001020"></a>
## vitalisation

*n.* 1. **賦予生命** — the state of being vitalized and filled with life

<a id="entry-v-001021"></a>
## vitality

**發音：** /vaɪˈtælɪti/
*n.* 1. **持續力, 活力, 生命力, 生存能力, 蓬勃的生氣** — a healthy capacity for vigorous activity
*“jogging works off my excess energy”*
*“he seemed full of vim and vigor”*
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **生命力** — The capacity to live and develop.
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **生命力** — Energy or vigour.
4. an energetic style
5. (biology) a hypothetical force (not physical or chemical) once thought by Henri Bergson to cause the evolution and development of organisms
6. the property of being able to survive and grow
*“the vitality of a seed”*

## vitalization

*n.* 1. **賦予生命** — the state of being vitalized and filled with life

2. **維生素 /維生素, 維他命 /維他命** — Any of a specific group of organic compounds essential in small quantities for healthy human growth, metabolism, development, and body function. They are found in minute amounts in plant and animal foods or sometimes produced synthetically, and deficiencies of specific vitamins produce specific disorders.

## vocabulary

4. a listing of the words used in some enterprise

*“he introduced a wide vocabulary of techniques”*

## vocal

*adj.* 1. **歌唱+的** — relating to or designed for or using the singing voice
*“vocal technique”*
*“the vocal repertoire”*
2. **聲+的, 聲音+的, 用嗓子發音+的, 口頭講+的, 口語+的** — having or using the power to produce speech or sound
*“vocal organs”*
*“all vocal beings hymned their praise”*
3. given to expressing yourself freely or insistently
*“outspoken in their opposition to segregation”*
*“a vocal assembly”*
4. full of the sound of voices
*“a playground vocal with the shouts and laughter of children”*
*n.* 1. music intended to be performed by one or more singers, usually with instrumental accompaniment
2. a short musical composition with words
*“a successful musical must have at least three good songs”*

2. either of two pairs of folds of mucous membrane projecting into the larynx

<a id="entry-v-001106"></a>
## vocal cords

<a id="entry-v-001107"></a>
## vocal fry

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **氣泡音 /氣泡音** — A low creaky vocal vibration caused by rapid vibration of the vocal chords, common in some languages such as Finnish. In English, often associated with older Received Pronunciation and more recent North American speech patterns.

## vocalis

**發音：** /voʊˈkeɪ.lɪs/

<a id="entry-v-001121"></a>
## vocative case

*n.* 1. **呼格** — case of address, case used for a noun identifying the person or thing being addressed. It corresponds to the archaic English particle "O" as used in solemn or poetic address e.g.: Hear me, O Albion! Languages that regularly employ the vocative include Arabic, Bengali, Bulgarian, Czech, Georgian, Greek, Hawaiian, Hindi-Urdu, Irish, Latin, Latvian, Lithuanian, Ojibwe, Polish, Romanian, Russian, Ruthenian/Rusyn, Sanskrit, Scottish Gaelic, Serbo-Croatian, Slovak, Tamil and Ukrainian.
2. the case (in some inflected languages) used when the referent of the noun is being addressed

*“the speaker was interrupted by loud cries from the rear of the audience”*

## voice

*n.* 1. **表達** — expressing in coherent verbal form

*“I gave voice to my feelings”*

*“the meeting was attended by spokespersons for all the major organs of government”*

*“he lost his voice”*
4. **語態** — (linguistics) the grammatical relation (active or passive) of the grammatical subject of a verb to the action that the verb denotes

*“A shrill voice sounded behind us”*
6. the sound made by the vibration of vocal folds modified by the resonance of the vocal tract
*“a singer takes good care of his voice”*

*v.* 1. give voice to

2. utter with vibrating vocal chords

<a id="entry-v-001133"></a>
## voicebank

<a id="entry-v-001134"></a>
## voiced

**發音：** /vɔɪst/
*adj.* 1. **有聲+的, 有濁音+的, 濁音+的** — produced with vibration of the vocal cords
*“a frequently voiced opinion”*
*“voiced consonants such as `b' and `g' and `z'”*
2. *(not-comparable)* **有聲 /有聲, 濁 /濁** — Sounded with vibration of the vocal cords. For example, the phone [z] is voiced, while [s] is unvoiced.

<a id="entry-v-001135"></a>
## voiceless

**發音：** /ˈvɔɪsləs/
*adj.* 1. **無聲+的, 清音+的** — produced without vibration of the vocal cords
*“unvoiced consonants such as `p' and `k' and `s'”*
2. *(not-comparable)* **無聲 /無聲, 清** — Spoken without vibration of the vocal cords; unvoiced, surd, breathed. Examples: [t], [s], [f], [m̥], [u̥].
3. deprived of the rights of citizenship especially the right to vote
*“labor was voiceless”*
*“disenfrenchised masses took to the streets”*
4. uttered without voice
*“could hardly hear her breathed plea, `Help me'”*
*“voiceless whispers”*
5. being without sound through injury or illness and thus incapable of all but whispered speech

<a id="entry-v-001136"></a>
## voicelessness

*n.* 1. **發音不能, 失聲** — a disorder of the vocal organs that results in the loss of voice
2. speaking softly without vibration of the vocal cords
3. having no voice in the management or control of affairs
*“the voicelessness of those who live in situations of hopelessness”*

<a id="entry-v-001137"></a>
## voicemail

*n.* 1. *(broadly, countable)* **語音信箱 /語音信箱, 話音郵件 /話音郵件** — An individual message on such a system.
2. a computerized system for answering and routing telephone calls; telephone messages can be recorded and stored and relayed

<a id="entry-v-001138"></a>
## voicing

**發音：** /ˈvɔɪsɪŋ/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **清濁 /清濁** — A classification of speech sounds that tend to be associated with vocal cord vibration.
2. the act of adjusting an organ pipe (or wind instrument) so that it conforms to the standards of tone and pitch and color

<a id="entry-v-001139"></a>
## void

**發音：** /vɔɪd/
*adj.* 1. **無效力+的, 作廢+的, 無效+的** — lacking any legal or binding force
*“null and void”*
2. *(not-comparable)* **無效** — Of no legal force or effect, incapable of confirmation or ratification.
3. containing nothing
*“the earth was without form, and void”*
*n.* 1. **空虛** — An empty space; a vacuum.
2. **不存在** — the state of nonexistence

*v.* 1. **宣告無效, 撤消, 使無效, 取消** — declare invalid

*“The concert hall was voided of the audience”*
4. take away the legal force of or render ineffective
*“invalidate a contract”*
5. excrete or discharge from the body

## vote

**發音：** /vəʊt/
*v.* 1. **投票決定, 表決, 投票** — express one's preference for a candidate or for a measure or resolution; cast a vote
*“He voted for the motion”*
*“None of the Democrats voted last night”*
2. **選舉, 投票** — express one's choice or preference by vote
*“vote the Democratic ticket”*
3. **選舉, 表決** — express a choice or opinion
*“I vote that we all go home”*
*“She voted for going to the Chinese restaurant”*
4. *(intransitive)* **投票, 表決 /表決** — To cast a vote; to assert a formalized choice in an election.
5. **表決, 投票** — be guided by in voting
*“vote one's conscience”*
6. **投票選舉, 投票決定** — bring into existence or make available by vote
*“They voted aid for the underdeveloped countries in Asia”*
*n.* 1. **投票選舉, 抽籤, 表決, 選舉, 投票** — a choice that is made by counting the number of people in favor of each alternative
*“there were only 17 votes in favor of the motion”*
*“they allowed just one vote per person”*
2. **參政權, 投票, 選舉權** — a legal right guaranteed by the 15th amendment to the US Constitution; guaranteed to women by the 19th amendment
*“American women got the vote in 1920”*
3. **投票, 表決 /表決** — An act or instance of participating in such a choice, e.g., by submitting a ballot.
4. the opinion of a group as determined by voting
*“they put the question to a vote”*
5. a body of voters who have the same interests
*“he failed to get the Black vote”*
6. the total number of voters who participated
*“they are expecting a large vote”*

<a id="entry-v-001235"></a>

*“The faculty turned thumbs down on the candidate for the Dean position”*

<a id="entry-v-001290"></a>
## vulture

**發音：** /ˈvʌltʃə/
*n.* 1. **兀鷹 /兀鷹, 鵰 /雕** — Any of several carrion-eating birds of the families Accipitridae and Cathartidae.
2. any of various large diurnal birds of prey having naked heads and weak claws and feeding chiefly on carrion
3. someone who attacks in search of booty

<a id="entry-v-001291"></a>
## vulture capitalism

*n.* 1. *(informal, uncountable)* **兀鷲投資 /兀鷲投資** — The practice of acquiring distressed firms in the hopes of making them more profitable so as to sell them for a profit.

<a id="entry-v-001292"></a>
## vulture capitalist

*n.* 1. *(derogatory)* **兀鷲投資者 /兀鷲投資者** — A takeover artist who loads a venture up with debt while extracting capital for profit, making the firm go bankrupt.
